### 2026-08-26 (MVD 18:04) | DECISION-V4-12 | EMA weight-convention audit + adoption; prereg v3->v4; trainer materializes EMA pre-LoRA (CPU, 0 cr)

* **Trigger (Gabriel):** the CPU e2e "green" bypassed `generate_sample` (hence `ema_scope`), hiding a structural EMA problem. `LitEma` freezes its param-name map at construction (BEFORE LoRA injection); after PEFT, `copy_to` KeyErrors on lora_A/lora_B and never applies EMA to the renamed base — runtime `ema_scope` is INVALID post-PEFT. Underneath sits a scientific question: raw vs EMA weights define different models.
* **Audit** (`scripts/research/ema_convention_audit.py`): upstream inference uses EMA (`use_ema=True` default at ddpm.py:73, NOT overridden by config; `generate_sample`→`ema_scope`). raw model.diffusion_model.* vs the EMA shadow are **materially different**: dense 690/690 tensors differ (mean-rel **2.4%**, max-rel 20%); p1_recovered 690/690 differ (mean-rel **5.1%**, max-rel 54%). **p1_pruned's stored EMA is BROKEN**: 238 pruning-affected tensors are DENSE-shaped (960) while raw is pruned (192) — Arshdeep pruned the raw U-Net but not its EMA.
* **Decision (V4-12): ONE convention = EMA (inference) weights, materialized into the ordinary U-Net BEFORE LoRA injection, runtime EMA disabled.** Per backbone: dense = materialize its model_ema (690/690 clean); **p1_pruned = (1,2,3,1) L1 selection of the DENSE EMA** (its stored EMA is unusable; pruning the dense EMA is the EMA-consistent inference backbone, same L1 selection applied to EMA weights); p1_recovered = materialize its own model_ema (690/690 clean).
* **Demonstrated CPU-side** (`scripts/research/ema_convention_demo.py` PASS): dense EMA materializes clean and changes the U-Net OUTPUT by max|Δ|≈5.9 vs raw (NOT negligible); p1_pruned stored EMA unusable→pruned-from-dense-EMA forwards@96; p1_recovered EMA materializes clean, output differs by ≈5.7, forwards@96. Helper `research_pruning/eval/ema_weights.py` (`ema_unet_state_dict`, `materialize_ema_into_unet`).
* **Applied:** prereg VERSIONED v3→v4 (`weight_convention` block; v3 preserved sha `8f7d426b…`). **Trainer materializes dense EMA before LoRA + sets `model.use_ema=False`**; dry-run PASS (ema_convention_applied=690/0/use_ema-off, all prior assertions still green). This changes which weights Gate 0 trains against (dense EMA, not raw) — PRE-DATA correction. B8 still 8/8. No GPU. 0 cr.

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| CLAP (frozen V1.1) | ↑ | 0.204 | 0.100 | 0.098 | −0.0024 | [−0.027, +0.021] rec≈pruned |
| Human-CLAP (frozen) | ↑ | 0.392 | 0.229 | 0.256 | +0.028 | [−0.012, +0.068] incl. 0 |
| PANN top-10 capture | ↑ | 0.446 | 0.339 | 0.362 | +0.023 | [−0.044, +0.088] incl. 0 |
| KL (softmax) | ↓ | 2.852 | 3.424 | 3.358 | −0.067 | [−0.396, +0.251] incl. 0 |
| FD PANN-2048 | ↓ | 71.1 | 78.4 | 80.8 | +2.4 | descriptive (n=96); rec ≈/slightly worse |
| FAD VGGish | ↓ | 8.83 | 14.53 | 14.70 | +0.17 | descriptive (r0/r1 8.83… rec≈pruned) |

* **PANN capture:** recall dense 0.446 [0.372,0.520] ≫ pruned 0.339 [0.276,0.403] ≈ recovered 0.362 [0.303,0.418]. Δrec−pruned +0.023 [−0.044,+0.088] (incl. 0). frac(rec>pruned)=0.302 but frac(rec==pruned)=0.417 — ties dominate (median GT size 2), so the recall point-Δ is the better summary, not the win-fraction. rec−dense −0.084 [−0.166,−0.006] and pruned−dense −0.107 [−0.174,−0.041] (both exclude 0): pruning loses capture, recovery does NOT restore it here.

* **INTERPRETATION → Pattern B (NOT A).** (1) Metric-axis dependence is **not** a credible explanation of the V1.1 CLAP null: the authors' FAD/KL/capture do NOT favor recovered either — recovered≈pruned is **metric-invariant** at this operating point, not a CLAP artifact.   on the eligible requested events. Primary per-severity estimand is
  `T_s = mean_late(rec-pruned) - mean_early(rec-pruned)`, with the boundary fixed at the prior 3.84-s
  operating point inside each existing 10.24-s waveform. `B=10000`, prompt unit, new seed namespace.
  Promotion requires positive point estimates at both severities and the severity-2 95% CI excluding

  pass first. If compatible and separately authorized, generate only this system on the frozen Arm-D
  80 prompts at 3.84 and 10.24 s = **160 new WAVs**; reuse all dense comparators. Primary references:

### 2026-09-01 (MVD 00:24) | LISTENING-STUDY-ESTIMATOR-FIX-V1.2 | Unique-prompt primary estimator (pre-participant; CPU, 0 GPU, no human data)

* **Issue (supervisor audit):** the v1.1 primary estimator (listener-stratified prompt bootstrap) placed every listener rating into listener strata, so the 18 D3 bridge prompts (rated by 2 listeners) contributed TWICE to H1/H2 — violating the project's prompt=statistical-unit principle. It was also mildly liberal (fixed-panel null Type-I ≈ 0.039–0.043 vs nominal 0.025). Detected pre-launch; no human responses existed; manuscript frozen; no GPU; no new generation.
* **PRE-PARTICIPANT AMENDMENT v1.2** (`docs/listening_study_protocol_v1_2_amendment.md`; v1 `aa906c2` + v1.1 `a6c46a3` immutable). protocol_hash = sha256(v1 base ++ v1.1 amendment ++ v1.2 amendment).
* **New PRIMARY estimand = UNIQUE-PROMPT weighting:** `H_{i,d}` = mean signed recovered-preference over ALL of prompt i's assigned raters at duration d (non-bridge: 1 rating; bridge: mean of its 2), so each of the 80 unique sev-1 prompts contributes EXACTLY once. `A_native=(1/80)Σ_i H_{i,native}` (H1, lo95>0); `J_H=(1/80)Σ_i(H_{i,native}−H_{i,short})` (H2, gated on H1, lo95>0). **Unique-prompt bootstrap** (resample 80 unique prompt records), B=10000, ns `LISTENING-STUDY|HUMAN-BOOTSTRAP|V1.2|2026-08-31`, 95% percentile. Frozen in `scripts/research/listening_analyze.py` (fails closed unless 80 unique / 18 two-rater / 62 one-rater; primary uses experimental sev1 only, ignores catch; self-test Type-I 0.013 / power 1.0).
* **FIXED-PANEL target retained** ("our six-listener expert panel"; naive-generalization null inflates Type-I to 0.18–0.23 for every estimator).
* **Calibration/power sim v3** (`scripts/research/listening_power_sim_v3.py`; `configs/research/listening_study_power_v3.json` self-sha `02d5c2b6…`) implements the exact deployed D3 estimator and compares 3 estimators: **unique-prompt** fixed-panel Type-I **0.019**, J-null (H1∧H2) **0.026** (both ≤ nominal) — well-calibrated; listener-stratified 0.039/0.038 (liberal, double-weights bridge); pooled-all 0.021/0.023. Power (unique-prompt): H1 anchor 0.982 / conserv 0.816 / stress 0.956; H1∧H2 anchor 0.641 / conserv 0.256 / stress 0.597. Adopted unique-prompt per the fixed criterion (calibration + correct estimand over the stratified estimator's small, inflation-driven power gain). Type-I 0.019 comfortably below the ~0.03–0.035 STOP threshold.
* **Bridge role:** primary = averaged within prompt (no double weight); secondary = non-gating inter-rater agreement (signed, exact/within-1, disagreement) on 18 prompts × 2 durations. **Sensitivities (non-gating, cannot rescue a gate):** v1.1 listener-stratified; pooled-all-ratings; leave-one-listener-out (drop a listener's ratings, retain a prompt if ≥1 rating remains, report N, recompute unique-prompt estimator, NO imputation); bridge agreement.
* **Receiver README payload** corrected to v1.2 (removed stale `type`; added `completed_A/completed_B`); receiver code already independent of `type`.

Loudness pair audit re-run: **NEGLIGIBLE** (signed rec−pru overall +0.0025 dB). **Internal QA 102/102 PASS** (`listening_study_validation.json`), incl. 80-unique-prompt structure, analyzer-ignores-catch, README payload contract, both-playback enforcement, no public leak, loudness pair-audit negligible; est. ≤16.8 min.
